HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0394e8f6f1e82b8462dd","text":"1. **Encounter with fire completes and stamps the flame-identity.** **Lexical evidence:** `يَصْلَى` has root `ص ل ي`, branch `B003` (entering, suffering, or otherwise encountering fire and heat); `نَارًا` has root `ن و ر`, branch `B002` (burning fire, including branding by it); and `لَهَب` has root `ل ه ب`, branch `B001` (visible blazing flame). Earlier `لَهَب` in `111:1` carries branch `B006` as an epithet. **Structure:** the future prefix moves the earlier flame-name toward a fire whose defining attribute repeats that name. **Role and abduction:** encounter, branding, epithet, and flame are supplied; I infer that the fire inscribes onto the person the identity previously carried only as a designation. **Reading change:** the sentence is not generic exposure to fire but the material completion of a named identity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0546a652d36503f847e1","text":"2. **The fire is maintained by a visible supply mechanism.** **Lexical evidence:** root `ص ل ي`, branch `B004`, includes kindling, fuel, and using fire; `ل ه ب`, branch `B001`, includes ignition; `111:4` then supplies `حَطَب`, root `ح ط ب`, branch `B001` (firewood gathered as fuel), borne by `حَمَّالَة`, root `ح م ل`, branch `B001` (an outward load). **Structure:** fire precedes carrier and fuel, so the following ayah reveals the material input implicit in the blaze. **Role and abduction:** the packet gives fire, carrier, and fuel; I supply the ordinary causal arrow that carried wood feeds and prolongs ignition. **Reading change:** `نَارًا ذَاتَ لَهَب` becomes a sustained household production chain rather than an isolated fiery scene.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_111b3d5dce3ba3c18e21","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:3:1\",\"root_000880\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Aynı düzen, ateşi kişiyi tüketip yıkımını sabitleyen bir işleme süreci olarak okumayı da önerir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_14f669a84a8c617b3bce","text":"`111:4` supplied both the ordinary fuel that sustains the blaze and the tale-bearing branch that activates `ن و ر` branch `B007`, so the fire acquired physical and social causal mechanisms at once. `111:5` was the sharper surprise: shared snare imagery in `ص ل ي` branch `B005` and `ح ب ل` branch `B005` corrected a reading of mere fire exposure into one of enforced, inescapable exposure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B004","root_001379/B001","root_001564/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1711b169e824f86ee2f2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:3:1\",\"root_000880\",[\"B004\"]],[\"111:3:2\",\"root_001564\",[\"B002\"]],[\"111:3:4\",\"root_001379\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sonraki taşıyıcı ve odun, alevin tek bir sahne değil, yakıt sağlanarak sürdürülen bir hane mekanizması olduğunu gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2ea6fce58fd65304babe","text":"4. **A social blaze is made materially visible.** **Lexical evidence:** `نَارًا`, root `ن و ر`, activates branch `B007` (enmity flaring among people); `حَطَب` in `111:4`, root `ح ط ب`, branch `B003`, is tale-bearing and stirring harm between people. The explicit fire sense remains supplied by `ن و ر` branch `B002` and `ل ه ب` branch `B001`. **Structure:** the ayah of fire is immediately followed by a carrier whose “wood” has an accepted social-harm branch. **Role and abduction:** the packet supplies enmity and tale-bearing; I infer the directional arrow that transmitted speech supplies fuel for hostility. **Reading change:** the fire reads concurrently as physical blaze and as social conflict brought to combustion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B003","root_001379/B001","root_001564/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_49c91a7a4cd493899d57","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:3:1\",\"root_000880\",[\"B003\"]],[\"111:3:2\",\"root_001564\",[\"B002\"]],[\"111:3:4\",\"root_001379\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ateş, kişinin alev adını dışarıdan eklenen bir cezadan çıkarıp onun üzerinde maddi olarak tamamlanan ve damgalanan bir kimliğe dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000335/B003","root_001379/B003","root_001564/B002","root_001564/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6ee1ed46aca8351c72f9","text":"4. **Fire as visible branding and hostile flare.** Branch evidence: `ن و ر` B002, burning fire and branding by it; `ن و ر` B007, enmity flaring between people; `ل ه ب` B003, rising glare/dust/smoke like flame; later `ح ط ب` B003, tale-bearing/stirring harm. Structural cue: `نَارًا` is followed by `ذَاتَ لَهَب`, then the next ayah supplies a carrier of kindling or slander. Assigned role: fire makes social hostility visible as a mark and glare. Abductive move: I infer that the later tale-bearing/fuel image explains what kind of \"fire\" this is socially, while the fire branch keeps it materially hot. Reading change: the fire reads as both punishment and the visible flare of enmity that has been fed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ba0596092d673d8a676d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:3:1\",\"root_000880\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Son boyun bağı, ateşle karşılaşmayı yalnız ısıya maruz kalma değil, kaçışı engelleyen bir yakalanma düzeni olarak derinleştirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B003","root_001379/B001","root_001379/B006","root_001564/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bd5042cfc99645e22279","text":"1. **The fire is continuous with the name, not an external add-on.** Branch evidence: earlier `ل ه ب` B006, name/epithet from flame; here `ل ه ب` B001, blazing flame; `ن و ر` B002, burning fire; `ص ل ي` B003, encountering/entering/suffering fire. Structural cue: the same root `ل ه ب` moves from the man's title in 111:1 to the fire's attribute in 111:3. Assigned role: the fire actualizes the lexical matter already present in the social name. Abductive move: I infer that repetition creates identity between the named man and the fiery medium he meets. Reading change: the ayah reads as an unveiling of a latent name-reality, not merely as a new punishment scene.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B002","root_000880/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_bf69af81c4ae3d617ebe","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:3:1\",\"root_000880\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Dua ve esenlik imkânını taşıyan aynı anlam alanı, burada ateşle temas biçiminde tersine dönmüş bir ilişki olarak duyulabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d4ba15e6595efee96ed4","text":"3. **The fire is also a capture apparatus.** **Lexical evidence:** root `ص ل ي`, branch `B005`, names snares and traps; `111:5` has `حَبْل`, root `ح ب ل`, branch `B005` (a catching, entangling snare) and branch `B001` (a cord for binding or leading). **Structure:** future encounter with fire is followed by a rope fixed at the neck, replacing free movement with restraint. **Role and abduction:** the two trap branches and bodily placement are supplied; I infer that the cord enforces continued exposure by blocking escape. **Reading change:** `سَيَصْلَى` can carry not only thermal contact but inescapable emplacement within the fire system.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000291/B005","root_000880/B002","root_000880/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_dfeef53456e9be6cb118","text":"- 111:4 was the main retrospective activation: the fire in 111:3 is not isolated, because the next ayah supplies a carrier and firewood. This made `ص ل ي` B004, kindling and fire-processing, much stronger than a simple \"encountering heat\" reading. - 111:5 added a trap structure. With `ح ب ل` B005 and `ص ل ي` B005 both containing snare/trap imagery, `سَيَصْلَىٰ` can be heard as being brought into a capture-process, not only arriving at a location. - The basmalah retroactively made the unused `ص ل ي` B002 branch noticeable. Because the packet opens with mercy, the ṣ-l-y branch of blessing/mercy stands as a blocked alternative behind the fire-contact branch.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000172/B002","root_000335/B001","root_000880/B003","root_000880/B004","root_001422/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e595dd25b43b48442dff","text":"2. **Entering fire also means being processed by fire.** Branch evidence: `ص ل ي` B003, suffering fire; `ص ل ي` B004, kindling, roasting, and fire-straightening; earlier `ت ب ب` B002, becoming settled/straight/regular; later `ح ط ب` B001, firewood; `م س د` B001, firm twisting. Structural cue: `سَيَصْلَى` stands between repeated ruin and later fuel/rope material. Assigned role: fire is not only heat but an operation that consumes, straightens, and fixes. Abductive move: I supply the process arrow: the fire does work upon the person as later material images do work upon wood and rope. Reading change: \"he will enter fire\" becomes \"he will be put through a fire-process that completes and regularizes the ruin.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001379/B001","root_001379/B003","root_001564/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_eb24d8da2897b1723c50","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:3:2\",\"root_001564\",[\"B007\"]],[\"111:3:4\",\"root_001379\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ardından gelen zarar verici söz taşıma görüntüsü, ateşi insanlar arasında körüklenen düşmanlığın yükselen, görünür ve yakıcı biçimi yapar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B002","root_000880/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_fdfcdab41e7675a23e4b","text":"3. **A mercy/prayer branch is present by reversal.** Branch evidence: `ص ل ي` B002, supplication, blessing, mercy; earlier `ر ح م` B001, mercy; here `ص ل ي` B003, fire-encounter. Structural cue: the basmalah's mercy-root opens the packet; the same ṣ-l-y inventory includes prayer/blessing but the surface form here is tied to fire and `نَارًا`. Assigned role: the blessing branch supplies the blocked alternative relation with God. Abductive move: I infer that the packet lets the reader hear the unused blessing branch only because the local syntax chooses fire-contact. Reading change: the ayah is sharpened: the contact that might have been prayer/mercy is experienced as heat.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
